## Fix: dark water from the radiation shader swap (Claude Code session, 2026-11-01, shader agent; code in `RadiationShading.ShaderSwap`; built from mirror `shader` (HEAD + this change) as build 235 and installed; checked in game)

- **Report (YouTuber on 0.8.x, required mods only):** water "very, very dark", "more like black, definitely not blue"; badwater "much darker than it should be". The user saw it too.
- **Root cause (in game; evidence in docs/radiation.md "In game"):** assigning `Material.shader` drops a material's custom render queue. `PhysicalWater_Opaque` has custom queue 3000 (`resources.assets`; queue 3000 in the menu before any game); its shader's own queue is AlphaTest 2450. Under the variant it drew at 2450, in URP's opaque pass, before the camera colour and depth copies that its transparency and depth tint sample: clean water and badwater near-black. `ShaderSwap.Restore` put the native shader back but not the queue, so the dark water also outlived a return to the menu.
- **How it was found (same save, camera and time of day; `outputs/water-shading/<label>/`, side by side in `outputs/water-shading/comparison-232-233-235.png`):** build 232 (HEAD + probe) dark, also with the native shader restored in-process; mirror-only build 233 with the swap skipped: blue; build 234 experiments: variant never assigned (radiation textures, uploads and keywords still running) blue, variant assigned and restored before the first frame dark. Ruled out first: the compiled variants (opaque water ForwardLit DXBC byte-identical to native with the radiation keyword off; transparent water and terrain differ only in `$Globals` layout; properties, defaults, keyword sets and pass states equal), the material property values (all equal to `resources.assets`), keyword stripping, and the forced-on radiation path with no radiation.
- **Fix:** `ShaderSwap` records the native material's `renderQueue` and puts it back after every shader assignment, variant or native, and throws if it does not hold. Materials already at the new shader's queue (terrain 2450, transparent water 3000) are left untouched. `SceneCycleAcceptance` now records and compares render queues (the build-223 scene-cycle run had passed with the queue already lost); `validate_radiation_content.mjs` requires the restore (fails with it removed).
- **New tool:** `tools/start_water_shading.ps1` with `Scripts/Radiation/WaterShadingProbe.cs` (`-deepFrontierWaterShading <label>`): variant, native and forced-keyword captures of clean water and badwater, then radioactive water and soil radiation, with the material state logged. `RadiationRenderer.Swaps` exposes the swaps to it.
- **In game, build 235:** clean water centre RGB 60,69,70 (was 45,48,45; 54-60,66-69,69-70 without the swap), badwater 55,48,42 (was 44,44,40; 55,48,42 without the swap); the native and forced-keyword captures equal the variant capture within moving-water noise; terrain unchanged; radioactive water bright yellow (135,140,63, was dark green) with yellow-green soil veins beside it. Scene cycle `-Label 235 -Cycles 1` COMPLETE: the save and a new Canyon game drew the variants at queues 3000/3000/2450, and both menus found all seven materials as before the first game, render queue included. Memory Test was restored exactly afterwards (21 files, same bytes and times; the runs' autosaves removed).
- **Checks:** compile check 0 errors; `validate_all -Only radiation_content` passes in the mirror.
- **Not verified:** a Folktails or map-editor scene (same native materials); other graphics settings (high-quality water off, the transparent-water toggle); a full `-Cycles 2` scene cycle. A game session started before the update keeps the lost queue until Timberborn restarts.

## Building tooltips: vanilla-length descriptions, one pipe clause, plain map notice (Claude Code session, 2026-11-01; generated and validated in mirror `descriptions`; not built or run in game)

- **User:** a YouTuber said our building tooltips "take half the screen" and the texts are confusing (Deep Well "1,667 hp at 1×", the Human Record Cache, deposits, the startup fishing notice). Descriptions must be concise, clear and to the point.
- **Contract (new `tools/validate_building_descriptions.mjs`):** every `Building.*.Description` at most 200 characters, mean at most 120 (vanilla Iron Teeth: max 154, mean 57, read from the vanilla localization in the linked vendor folder), no "production building" placeholder, no "×" notation; flavor texts at most 100; the four tooltip sections Deep Frontier adds before placement (smog emitter planned + mitigation, scrubber planned, cryogenic rules) at most 100. It fails on HEAD's localization (95 failures) and passes on the new one.
- **Result:** 98 building descriptions, mean 260.7 -> 109.6 characters (median 226 -> 115), longest 1,112 (Nuclear Reactor) -> 198. Numbers the native tooltip shows (cost, science, workers, power, recipes) left the prose; the risk a player must know stays in one sentence (reactor meltdown, vault and refinery leaks, Deep Well drought power, map edge for the export, open water under the recovery pump intake, trained crews).
- **Producers (no override layer):** `create_frontier_expansion_content.mjs` `BUILDING_TEXT` now holds authored text for every expansion building and throws on a building without one (the "Deep Frontier X production building" fallback is gone; 13 buildings shipped with it). Also `create_vertical_slice_content.mjs` (deep drill/mine/processor, Deep Ore Source and Vein), `meltdown_content.mjs` (`PRODUCER_DESCRIPTIONS`, `descriptions()`), `rocket_program_content.mjs` (`GENERIC_PAD_DESCRIPTION` is the expansion seed), `hospital_building_content.mjs`, `deep_well_content.mjs` (new `DROUGHT_SUMMARY`; the panel keeps `DROUGHT_RULE`), `super_bot_content.mjs`, `narrative_content.mjs`, `radiation_content.mjs`, and the catalogs `construction_materials.json`, `structural_content.json`, `hydro_content.json`, `research_content.json`.
- **Appended clauses:** `connection_content.mjs` `PIPE_PHRASES` are one clause each: "Pipes: valve set to Inlet." / "... Outlet." / "Pipes: valves set to Inlet and Outlet." / "Pipes: connect directly." (`pipePhrase` throws on an unknown port state). `bot_upkeep_content.mjs` `upkeepDescription`: "Wears out metal block drill bits; stops without them." (the rates are in the Upkeep panel).
- **Tooltip sections:** `DeepFrontier.Smog.Emitter.Planned` now takes radius and penalty only (`SmogEmitter.cs` passes two arguments; the smokestack count is gone), Mitigation, Scrubber.Planned and `DeepFrontier.Cryogenics.Describe.Rules` shortened; steel leg deck live lines (Blocked, Columns, PointAt) shortened. Other `IEntityDescriber` sections (smog on finished buildings, intake water shares, irradiated planting, zombie) only show on placed entities and were left.
- **Map notice (`tools/map_setup.json`):** `UnmetTitle` says the map is short of a few things and the game can still be finished; every `unmetText` item now names what is missing, what it affects and what to do (fishing: dam a clean river to 0.5 m beside a dry bank; it feeds the hatchery, canned fish and fish oil). Sites: "No ready space for: ... Level ground or build on platforms". `docs/maps.md` and `docs/platforms-design.md` quote the new texts.
- **Validators updated, not dropped:** `validate_deep_well_content.mjs` checks the description carries `DROUGHT_SUMMARY` (with the drought hp and "dries up") and no "×"; meltdown, connection, bot upkeep and rocket checks read the producers' exports and pass unchanged. Only English rows exist (enUS.csv, enUS_cryogenics.csv, enUS_smog.csv); keys unchanged.
- **Checks (mirror `descriptions`):** canonical generation reaches a fixed point and changes only the three localization files (121 rows of enUS.csv, one cryogenics row, three smog rows); `validate_all -Only` (19 checks: building_descriptions, bot_upkeep, connection_faces, construction_materials, cryogenic_content, deep_well_content, map_setup, meltdown_content, narrative_content, radiation_content, restaurant_content, rocket_program, smog_content, steel_leg_decks, super_bots, vertical_slice_content, workforce_risks, tool_groups, automation_content) pass; compile check 0 errors (3 warnings, as before). Full `validate_all -SkipBlender` (run after another agent had started reworking the Steel Mill, Fertilizer Works, Cement Kiln, Grocery and Hospital art in the shared tree, which the mirror syncs): 63 pass, 1 report-only, 3 fail: `smog_content` (SteelMill smoke mouth) and `vertical_slice_content` (FertilizerWorks footprint), both from that in-progress art and both passing in the targeted run on HEAD plus this change, and `no-game-assets` (no git in a mirror; passes on this commit's index).

## Roof hardpoints: steel legs on roofs that are not flat (Claude Code session, 2026-10-31, platforms agent; slice 3 of docs/platforms-design.md; generated and validated in mirror `legs` on 9a63247; not built or run in game)

- **User (2026-10-29, again 2026-10-31):** legs that "connect to and sit on top of non flat topped buildings (going through/into the building)".
- **Rule (`tools/roof_hardpoint_content.mjs`, list `tools/roof_hardpoints.json`, generated):** the top cell of every building column that is no flat roof deck, not family-stackable, has Top occupation, is not below BaseZ, has an exterior non-access cell above, nothing standing over it or hanging above, no animated part in it or above (new measurement `tools/asset-pipeline/measure_roof_hardpoints.py` -> `outputs/roof-hardpoints`), and no emitter or prop. Structural pieces and the Deep Well (`CLOSED`) get none. Applied after the roof decks: Stackable, `RoofHardpointsSpec {Cells}`, `GenerateFloorsOnStackable`.
- **Result:** 1,124 hardpoints on 47 templates; with the 544 flat roof cells, 93.5% of the 1,784 building top cells can carry a leg. The other 116: 68 animated, 9 emitters, 12 hatchery pen cells below BaseZ, 6 porch floors, 6 with geometry above, 2 Deep Well, 1 enclosed, 12 flat roofs the roof-deck step excludes. **D2 (sleeves through buildings) not built:** the remaining cells are where a column must not stand.
- **Code (`Scripts/SteelStructures/RoofHardpoints.cs`, no Harmony):** `RoofHardpointValidator` refuses every preview without `SteelSupportSpec` (on steel columns, steel decks, leg deck tools) whose foundation rests on a hardpoint; `HardpointSleeve` shows each column's `#HardpointSleeve` (the H1 column model one layer down) on a hardpoint; the leg deck preview draws the same sleeve. The slice 2 planner finds hardpoints as beds with no change.
- **Other files:** `validate_pit_exports.py` accepts roof decks plus hardpoints; `validate_automation_content.mjs` kind `placement`; `CivicFixtureGeometry.DeckStands` and `AllBuildingsSites.BuildOnDeck` skip hardpoints. Native floors over hardpoints are kept (an object cannot block its own stackable floors; builders need them to reach roof columns).
- **Checks (mirror `legs`):** canonical generation and both roof lists reach a fixed point (the roof-deck list's key order differs between mirror and repo traversal, entries equal, file untouched); new `validate_roof_hardpoints.mjs` and `validate_roof_hardpoint_exports.py` pass and fail on a broken blueprint; `validate_all -SkipBlender` 64 pass + 1 report-only (`no-game-assets` needs git; passes on the staged commit); compile check 0 errors.
- **In game, still to test:** docs/platforms-design.md section 6, items 13-18.

## Steel decks on legs: assisted placement, one Harmony postfix (Claude Code session, 2026-10-31, platforms agent; slice 2 of docs/platforms-design.md; generated and validated in mirror `legs` on b582d9b; not built or run in game)

- **User (2026-10-29, again 2026-10-31):** "dynamic leg length" for the bigger steel platforms: each of the four legs at its own height.
- **Player:** eight tools, `SteelLegDeck3x3H1`–`H4` and `SteelLegDeck5x5H1`–`H4` (Paths → Steel structures). The deck stands 1–4 layers above the pointed spot; one click places the real `SteelDeck` plus one `SteelColumnH*` stack per corner, each down to the first native bed, up to 12 layers (two stacked columns past 6). The preview draws those columns (clones of the column models); a leg that is blocked or finds no bed turns the preview red with a reason. The tool panel adds a live section with this spot's columns and their summed cost.
- **Native route:** tool blueprints (`structural_building_content.mjs` `legDeckBlueprints`, catalog `structural_content.json` `legDecks`) carry `SteelDeckToolSpec`; `SteelDeckPlacer` is a native `IBlockObjectPlacer` that creates every entity through the native `BuildingPlacer.Place`; leg lengths come from native `BlockValidator.BlocksValid`/`MatterBelowValidator` on the exact column blocks (`SteelLegPlanner`). The tool has one natively valid foot block under the cursor so `IsAlmostValid` keeps a failing preview visible. Everything placed is an ordinary building; the tool is never an entity.
- **Harmony (one):** `SteelDeckPlacerClaim`, a postfix on `BuildingPlacer.CanHandle` returning false for `SteelDeckToolSpec` templates, because `BlockObjectPlacerService.GetMatchingPlacer` takes `SingleOrDefault` and BuildingPlacer claims every `BuildingSpec`. Verified at mod start (callees of `CanHandle`, `SingleOrDefault` in `GetMatchingPlacer`).
- **Source facts found:** the game scene binds `DummyUndoRegistry` (no undo in game, only in the map editor); `IsAlmostValid` ignores validators, so an all-`Any` tool would be hidden instead of shown red; an object's `BlockedEdges` cannot remove its own stackable floor edges (`CanAddBlockingEdge`).
- **Checks (mirror `legs`):** canonical generation reaches a fixed point (only the 8 tool folders, `enUS.csv` and the building collection change; `material_cost_context.json` regenerated); new `validate_steel_leg_decks.mjs` passes and fails on a broken tool; `validate_all -SkipBlender` 62 pass + 1 report-only (`no-game-assets` cannot run without git in a mirror; it passes on the staged commit); compile check 0 errors. 7×7 not built (docs/platforms-design.md 4.4).
- **In game, still to test:** docs/platforms-design.md section 6.

I want it to be like an actual platform where roads can connect. buildings can be built on edges of two buildings butting up to each other, etc...." and "the resturaunt chairs and tables are a tad too tall, and beavers heads are clipping the ceiling when they dine."
- **Civic (8bdcef3, bb8e0f9, d26e8eb):** Restaurant, Materials Laboratory, Reinforced Hydro Turbine, Grocery, Hospital, Fish Hatchery, Fishery Works: every deck and roof rail, the Restaurant parapet and the turbine's edge curbs removed; `PlatformDeckSpec.Openings` is `[]` on all six deck buildings (door links kept; `PlatformDeck.cs` accepts an explicit empty list); acceptance fixtures derive deck cells from the Stackable blocks (`CivicFixtureGeometry.DeckStands/DeckSeams`), compiled, not run. The Materials Laboratory and Hydro Turbine models in Data had never been promoted from `outputs/*-integration` (the lab showed its pre-compaction model); both are promoted now. Connection faces kept or gained everywhere.
- **Restaurant seating:** a seated native beaver reaches 0.51 above its slot (BeaverAdult_sitting, measured locally from the game's model). Seats and slots 0.3375 -> 0.20 over the deck (the vanilla RooftopTerrace seat height), tabletops 0.6125 -> 0.475; seated head 0.71 under the 0.77 lower-balcony beams (+0.06, was -0.0775). Same tables, chairs and plan positions. `validate_civic_exports.py` checks the headroom per seat. A forester's hat (0.83) still touches the beams.
- **Late-game (707a3cb, 3578a57, 937228a, fc85f61, 165a272, f457f55):** parapets, curbs, deck, catwalk and tank-top rails gone from 30 studies; the helpers that made them (`parapet_ring`, `lategame_kit.parapet/catwalk/steel_rail/flat_roof(parapet_height)`, `civic_parts.rail/walk`, `nuclear_parts.ring_parapet`, `industry_parts.rail`, `space_parts.grating_ring`, `mining_parts.flat_roof(parapet)/deck(rails)/rail`, `compact_industry_parts` curbs) are deleted, so no default adds a parapet again. Steel Mill: the parapet had been the only cover of 13 melt-shop side faces; the walls now rise to a roof on the whole layer 4 (shaft faces 135 -> 138). Observation Deck telescope on its own pedestal. Kept, not on a roof: Water Treatment tower balcony ring, Launch Pad crew arm rails, Observation Deck stair handrail, ground-level kerbs and tank rims. Industrial family (crusher, kiln, mixer, quarries, pits) had no roof rails or parapets.
- **Navigation (source-confirmed 1.1.2.8, `NavMeshObjectUpdater` decompiled):** a Stackable roof block with `GenerateFloorsOnStackable` lays floor edges from the cell above to its four neighbours; auto walls only surround Bottom-occupied cells at their own layer. So abutting roofs join for player paths and a building can straddle the seam exactly when every roof cell, edge rows included, is Stackable with a free cell above. Rails were visual; their registered cells above roofs were what could break it.
- **Validator:** `flat_roof_platforms` now also runs each blueprint through that navigation model (`tools/roof_platform_nav.mjs`, with vanilla `Platform.IronTeeth` as the reference: two Platforms must link): roof-to-roof links within a building and seam links where another roof or a Platform can butt up at the same height must carry the floor edge both ways and not be blocked, with a valid straddling foundation; and a deck rail fails when either of its cells is a block that occupies Path (on the old HEAD: the 20 stale Restaurant rails plus FisheryWorks FishRight4-7). Now: 69 templates, 905 flat roof cells, 899 platformable, 6 under solid geometry, 1,100 roof links, 875 seam links on 617 edge cells, 0 rails.
- **Checks (mirror `roof_val` on f457f55 + the validator):** canonical generation reproduces the committed Data and `roof_decks.json` and reaches a fixed point; `validate_all -SkipBlender` 58 of 60 pass, `no-game-assets` passes in the repo (the mirror has no git), `audit-item-uses` report-only; triangle budget 845,434 / 872,890; compile check 0 errors. Per batch: strict z-fight 0 visible, 0 exposed hidden faces. Review sheets: `outputs/roofs/` (civic archive `docs/art/reviews/2026-10-31-roofs-civic`).
- **Open:** the Uranium Processing Plant fuel-vault plate deck tops at 2.032, so platforms sit 3 cm into it. The Restaurant sign gantry keeps 2 front-edge roof cells unplaceable (animated sign).
- **In game, still to test:** paths joining across two abutting roofs and onto stairs; a building straddling two abutting roofs (build, save, reload); every roof edge cell taking a Platform; the six deck buildings loading with no rails; diners' heads clear of the Restaurant ceiling; the Restaurant acceptance fixture past setup.

## Bricks and seven retired goods (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, bricks agent; data regenerated; validated in mirror `bricks`; not built or run in game yet)

- **User decisions (approved bricks proposal):** one new good, Brick, on existing buildings; seven low-value goods removed ("for every item we add, lets remove low value items from the game"); native save migration; Terrain Block later; costs follow materials.
- **Chain:** Stone Crusher `BrickEarth` 3 Sandstone -> 8 Dirt (1 h; 6 until art batch 3); Cement Kiln (900 -> 600 science) `FireBricks` 4 Dirt + 1 Water -> 4 Brick (2 h, 1 Log) and Cement 3 Limestone + 1 Dirt -> 2 Cement; Concrete Mixer 3 Sandstone + 2 Cement + 4 Water -> 8 Concrete (2 h). Brick and Cement at 600 (cumulative 2,200), Concrete at 1,000 (cumulative 3,500 -> 3,200). The kiln's Water input gives it Timber Pipe ports (connection rule: 32 -> 33 liquid templates).

## Built-in maps re-planned for the compacted footprints; smallMap.maxArea re-derived (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, mapreplan agent; variants, map setup data and docs; validated in mirror `mapreplan` on HEAD + this change; not built or run in game)

- **Why:** the compaction pass shrank the late-game footprints (Deep Mine and Deep Drill 7x6, quarries 8x6, reactor 14x11), so `map-setup-terrain` failed on `smallMap.maxArea 11176 != derived 5966` and many `unmet` notes cited the old sizes.
- **maxArea:** re-derived by `prepare_deep_frontier_map.py builtin` from the reference variants (largest claimed site area Plains 2,983 / land share 1/2) = 5,966. Small by area: Cliffside (5,000) and Diorama (2,500). No map lacks a Deep Mine site, so the EXTREMELY difficult list is Cliffside and Diorama; Beaverome drops off it. The dialog's reactor size reads 14 x 11 (`tools/map_setup.json` text.smallSpace).
- **Re-plan:** `plan` on every variant, plus a scratch driver around its `Planner` for what `plan` does not do (a second Deep Mine, a Deep Ore Source beside each new drill site, reactor sites with `maxWaterDistance` 15, moving smaller flat sites aside). `plan` itself aborts its flat stage with "a reactor site needs maxWaterDistance" when a reactor candidate reaches `check_site`; the variants carry the authored 15. Result: Beaverome 2 mines, 2 drills + 2 sources (8,500 each), Launch Pad and most industry; Diorama 2 mines; Cliffside quarry, pad, caches and rocket chain; reactor sites on Canyon, Craters, Hollows, Mountain Range, Oasis, Pillars, Spillage, Thousand Islands; Meander, Waterfalls, Pillars, Thousand Islands get their missing plants. Every remaining `unmet` reason was rewritten with current counts (positions on the empty map and the sites they overlap). Finite deep ore: 17,000 or 18,000 on every map against 11,528 needed.
- **Shipped map files:** the Plains, Plains Hard, Lakes and Terraces variants did not change and were not rebuilt. `validate` on the written files reports one error each, the description (it lists the 17 required mods from before 2feb3dc); they are to be rebuilt once with `build` after the 0.7.0 bump (coordinator).
- **Test fix:** `test_prepare_deep_frontier_map.py` case "footprint in an open margin" derives the vault depth from its blueprint (the 8x7 vault no longer reached the pad's margin); 17/17 caught.
- **Checks (mirror):** `prepare_deep_frontier_map.py builtin` passes on all 19 maps; `validate_map_setup` passes; `validate_all -SkipBlender` passes except `no-game-assets` (git is unavailable in a mirror).
- **In game, to check:** new games on Beaverome (no EXTREMELY difficult dialog, the shorter notice; 15 placements), Diorama and Cliffside (dialog text in `docs/maps.md`), and one reactor map (Canyon); see `docs/maps.md` "In-game test".

## Fewer required mods: Power Lines optional, no crash without the optional mods (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, dependencies agent; code, data and docs; official build from mirror `depfree` on HEAD cbaa4c4 plus this change, installed and run in game)

- **Player report (Workshop):** "without the following mods the game crashes upon loading the mods (listed as dependencies for your mod): Flywheels, Northforge Solar Panel, Expanded Storage, Teleporter Station, Power Lines, Powered Tubeway."
- **Reproduced (installed package before this change, only Deep Frontier and its required mods enabled, `outputs/memory-load-depfree-r1-new.log`):** a crash at mod load, before the main menu: `TypeLoadException: Could not load type of field 'DeepFrontier.Scripts.FactoryTransportAcceptance:_lines' ... 'PowerLines'`, from `ModCodeStarter.GetModStarters` (`IsAssignableFrom` over every type resolves field types on Mono). Behind it, `PowerLineSpec` on three blueprints would have thrown in SpecService `DeserializeUnsafe`. A missing RequiredMod by itself only warns. Details: docs/dependency-reference.md "Required and optional mods".
- **Fix:**
  - The Power Lines code (Factory Transport and Lategame acceptance harnesses) moved to the optional assembly `DeepFrontier.PowerLines` (`Scripts/PowerLines`, packaged `*.dll.PowerLines.optional`). The rocket harness and the shared records stay in the main assembly (`LategameRocketAcceptance.cs`). `InternalsVisibleTo` lets the harnesses keep using main-assembly fixtures. The build now packages every non-main asmdef `DeepFrontier.<ModId>` this way.
  - `PowerLineSpec` left the Mixer, Hydro Turbine and Materials Laboratory blueprints. The producers return it as an integration; `tools/optional_mod_content.mjs` writes `Data/Integrations/PowerLines/*.json`, and `PowerLineConnectionPoints` (the game's `IBlueprintModifierProvider`) adds it only with Power Lines.
  - Expanded Storage's pile patch is back as the native `PileModule.IronTeeth.optional.blueprint.json`.
  - Manifest: Power Lines moved from RequiredMods to the native `OptionalMods`, with Expanded Storage, Moddable Weathers and Sluice is Back!.
  - The vanilla Tubeway Station is hidden (`DevModeTool`, `tools/vanilla_tool_overrides.mjs`) in favour of New Building Styles' `TubewayStop.IronTeeth` (user decision).
  - `OptionalIntegrationsConfigurator` fails a package with Power Lines but no bridge, and a Power Lines acceptance launch without Power Lines.
- **Validator:** `tools/validate_mod_dependencies.mjs` (validate_all `mod_dependencies`), using the manifest, the asmdefs and the installed packages' DLL metadata (`tools/dotnet_metadata.mjs`): main sources use only required mods' namespaces, optional assemblies add only their own mod, blueprints carry no non-required mod's spec, optional patches and integrations target optional mods, and a hidden vanilla tool keeps a visible replacement from a required mod. Negative tests in the mirror caught `using PowerLines.Services`, a qualified ModdableWeathers type, `PowerLineSpec` back on the mixer, a non-optional PileModule patch and a hidden TubewayStop.
- **Checks:** canonical generation in mirror `depfree` (HEAD plus this change) changes exactly the three blueprints and adds the five new data files; validate_all `exports-*`, `model-bindings`, `automation_content`, `connection_faces`, `construction_materials`, `deep_frontier_manifest`, `mod_dependencies`, `vertical_slice_content` pass; both compile checks pass, and the main DLL no longer references PowerLines.
- **In game:** the mod prefs were backed up, set to only Deep Frontier plus its required mods, and restored afterwards; the registry matches the backup except for Unity session counters, and the Memory Test autosaves were restored byte-identical. Timberborn's own "Run in background" setting was on during the runs and is removed again.
  - Run 1, installed package before this change, required mods only: the crash above, at mod load (`outputs/memory-load-depfree-r1-new.log`).
  - Run 2, this build, required mods only: main menu, a new Plains game (map setup 19 of 19, 60 s, saved), a reload of that save, and an older full-mod save (`perf probe Day 30-7`) all loaded with no exceptions (`outputs/memory-load-depfree-r2-{new,load,oldsave}.log`). The old save's entities from absent mods (Solar Panel, Flywheels, Teleporter, Expanded Storage stores, Tubeway Levee and others) are skipped natively ("No template found"). The only other warnings are the two expected unknown keys of the standalone Moddable Weathers blueprint.
  - Run 3, this build, all of the user's mods: the same old save loads with no exceptions and no missing templates, the optional DLLs load, and the log shows the Power Lines connection points added to the mixer, the laboratory and the turbine (`outputs/memory-load-depfree-r3-all.log`).
  - The tested build predates e00f0dc (toolbar sub-groups claiming optional mods' templates), 054af59 and 5f4ccff; this commit sits on 5f4ccff, where generation, both compile checks and the touched validators pass again. A required-only run of that combined build is still to do.
  - Not inspected in game: the Expanded Storage pile patch and the hidden Tubeway Station (both native mechanisms, source-confirmed), and the moved acceptance harnesses (compile-checked only).

## Build menu sub-groups and storage order (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, toolbar agent; data regenerated; validated in mirror `toolbar` on HEAD 054af59 + this change; not built or run in game)

- **Player feedback (Workshop):** "opening the sub tabs makes some parts of the gui go out of screen". The player asked for sub-groups for the Iron Teeth suspension bridges and platforms, power shafts, flywheel types and dynamite, and for a consistent Storage order.
- **Mechanism (source-confirmed, Moddable Tool Groups 11.0.0; skill reference dependency-apis.md):**
  - A Deep Frontier group blueprint lists vanilla and other mods' templates in `ToolGroupChildrenSpec.ChildrenToolsTemplateNames` with `DoNotIncludePlaceableToolGroup`. No vanilla or other-mod blueprint is patched.
  - A missing template is skipped, and an empty group is never built. So a group of an optional mod's tools exists only with that mod, and without it nothing loads differently.
  - The native Storage group gets a same-path partial with `ChildrenOrderedIds#append`.
  - Group icons are lazy `AssetRef`s that are read only when a group's button is built.
- **Catalog:** `tools/tool_groups.json`, extended with:
  - `claims`, sub-groups of groups (`parent` may be a declared group), `nativeOrders`, and `externalMods`, which records the installed package each claimed template was read from.
  - Flywheels 4.1.1.15 keeps its blueprints in an asset bundle, and its template names swap Horizontal and Vertical against the display names; the groups follow the display names.
  - The generator (`tools/tool_group_content.mjs`) rejects a claim from another tab, a duplicate, an icon outside the group, or an optional-mod icon on a group that also shows without that mod.
  - `tools/validate_tool_groups.mjs` checks every claim and order against vanilla Resources, the generated Data and the installed Workshop packages: Id, Version, tool group and icon of each loose template, and the TemplateCollection for Flywheels' bundle. A required mod must be in RequiredMods; an optional one must not be. Each `nativeOrders` list must be complete. A planted typo and a wrong tool group both fail. The validator reports which groups are optional-only.
- **Top level per tab** (Iron Teeth, required mods only; [] = only with that optional mod):
  - **Paths, 14** (13 once the vanilla Tubeway Station is hidden, +1 with Teleporter Station; was 31 with the optional mods): Path, Stairs, Spiral Stairs, Platforms▸, Tubeway Station and New Styles' Tubeway Stop (both left alone: the dependency agent hides the vanilla station), Tubeways▸, Gate, Airlock, Suspension bridges▸, Overhangs▸, [Teleporters▸], Concrete scaffolds▸, Concrete side platforms▸, Steel structures▸.
  - **Power, 5** (was up to 26): Water power▸, Large Power Wheel, Power shafts▸ (with the Clutch), Engines and power plants▸ (Steam Engine, New Styles Steam Plant, Geothermal Engine, [Solar Panel], Coal, Oil, Nuclear), Gravity Battery, [Flywheels▸ (Inline / Horizontal framed / Vertical framed▸)]. With Power Lines, its Lantern pole stays where Power Lines puts it (Power, order 29).
  - **Landscaping, 13** (was 21): Dam, Levee, Floodgates▸, Fill Valve, Throttling Valve, Impermeable Floor, Irrigation Barrier, Explosives Factory, Dynamite▸, Tunnel, Dirt Excavator, Terrain Block, Masonry and concrete▸.
  - **Storage, 10** (16 with Expanded Storage): piles Small, Large, [Pile Module, Grand Underground]; warehouses Small, Medium, Large, [Storage Module, Grand], Radioactive Waste Vault; tanks Small, Medium, Large, [Tank Module, Grand], Cryogenic Tank Farm.
- **Checks (mirror, HEAD 054af59 + this change):**
  - Canonical generation reaches a fixed point. It changes only 9 ToolGroupIds, 15 new group blueprints, the Storage partial and 15 localization rows.
  - `validate_all -SkipBlender`: 54 of 58 pass. `map-setup-terrain` fails on `smallMap.maxArea` and `no-game-assets` fails in the mirror (both known). `radiation_content` fails on the `RadioactiveWaterSurface`/`IrradiatedSoilSurface.Unload` source regex, which is HEAD's shader code and not this change.
  - `validate_mod_dependencies` passes on the working tree.
- **In game, to check (not run yet):** see the screen-fit checklist in this entry's report. Open each tab at 1920x1080 and at 1280x720 with UI scale 100%. The top bar and every sub-group must stay on screen, including Power ▸ Flywheels ▸ a type (two levels raise the panel 108 px). Check the claimed tools build from their sub-group and are gone from the tab's top level, the Storage order, the optional groups appearing and disappearing with their mod, and no `[ConfigurableToolGroups]` warnings in Player.log.

## Deep Well drought rule; Lubricant merged into vanilla Grease (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, dwgrease agent; data regenerated; validated and compiled in mirror `dwgrease` on HEAD af9a3b8 + this change; not built or run in game)

- **Deep Well in droughts (player feedback "Deep well seems to be quite overpowered. Maybe remove the ability to get water through drought?"; user-approved: power gate plus reduced boost).** Numbers in `tools/deep_well_content.json` `drought`: `powerInputHp` 100,000, `maxBoost` 2 (normal: 10,000 hp at 6×).
  - **Drought state:** `DeepWell.DroughtActive` = native `WeatherService.IsHazardousWeather` and `HazardousWeatherService.CurrentCycleHazardousWeather.Id` equal to the injected native `DroughtWeather.Id` ("DroughtWeather"), the same test as the tide shut-off. Moddable Weathers (1.1 build, decompiled) replaces both services; its drought is `GameDroughtWeather` with the same id, and it keeps the native `DroughtWeather` bound. A Moddable Weathers weather that only carries its `DroughtModifier` under another id is not treated as a drought (not checked in game).
  - **Rule:** in a drought the native node demand is `DroughtPowerInput` at any boost (input multiplier 100,000 / 10,000), the well runs only at network power efficiency 1 (native `MechanicalGraph.PowerEfficiency` = min((supply + battery) / demand, 1)), and the boost and drill bits use min(Boost, 2). Short of full power it does not run, so the native drought modifier stays on and the source dries up; it keeps demanding 100,000 hp, so the panel says to pause it to free the power. New states `DroughtNoPower` and `DroughtBoosting`; the panel always shows the drought rule, and the building description states it. Seeps, tide shut-off and drill bits are unchanged.
  - **Checks:** `validate_deep_well_content.mjs` checks the numbers (at least 100,000 hp and above the normal draw; cap below 6×), the description and panel text, the spec fields and the C# drought logic.
- **Lubricant merged into vanilla Grease (user: "the vanilla game has grease. lets use it.").** Vanilla 1.1 `Good.Grease` (Iron Teeth `GoodCollection`, bot `Grease` need) is made by the vanilla `GreaseFactory.IronTeeth` recipe `Grease` (1 Extract + 1 CanolaOil → 2 Grease, 4 h; science 2,000), so Deep Frontier's `Lubricant` recipe at the Chemical Works (2 CrudeOil + 1 FishOil + 1 Water → 2) is removed with its stale Data file.
  - **Uses moved to Grease:** Cryocoolant (1 Polymer + 1 Grease + 2 Water), ServoBotLimbs (1 MachineCore + 1 Grease + 2 MetalPart) and bot maintenance (`workforce_risks.json`: Grease ×0.5 wear, FishOil ×0.75) on all 17 maintained workplaces. Item-use audit: Lubricant LEGACY SAVE-ONLY, CrudeOil and FishOil 2 uses each (minimum met), 0 of 57 flagged.
  - **Saves:** Lubricant is a `legacySaveOnlyGoods` / prohibited good in `content_policy.json` (the RecoveredMinerals pattern): defined, unproduced, unused, localized. Native `Inventory.Load` keeps saved stock of a no-longer-allowed good and `UnreservedUnwantedStock` lets haulers clear it; a Chemical Works saved on the old recipe loads with no recipe (native `Manufactory.Load`). Not run in game.
  - **Not changed:** `build_balance_workbook.mjs`, `tools/aquaculture_workbook.mjs` and `docs/brainstorm.md` keep their original design-era Lubricant rows.
- **Checks (mirror `dwgrease`, other agents' uncommitted files at HEAD):** canonical generation and `material_costs.mjs --write` reach a fixed point (no building cost changed); `validate_all -SkipBlender`: 54 pass, 1 report-only, 3 fail: `map-setup-terrain` (`smallMap.maxArea`, known), `no-game-assets` (no git in a mirror, known) and `radiation_content` (two `ShaderSwap` Unload checks on HEAD's radiation shading, untouched here). Compile checks (main and Moddable Weathers projects) build. **In game:** drought with and without 100,000 hp spare, the 2× cap, the panel states, a Moddable Weathers drought, and a save holding Lubricant.

## Fix: second game load in a session crashed in the radiation renderer; exception save before map setup threw (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, shader-fix agent; code 1ad77a8; build 223 installed by the coordinator; scene-cycle run in game: 3 of 4 scenes, all checks passed)

- **Crash (user, build 222):** loading a save, returning to the main menu, then starting a new game crashed in `RadiationRenderer.PostLoad` with "PhysicalWater_Opaque: expected native shader Shader Graphs/PhysicalWaterURP_Opaque, found Hidden/InternalErrorShader".
- **Root cause (source):** native `SceneLoader.LoadSceneCoroutine` calls `IAssetLoader.Reset` before `SceneManager.LoadScene`. The reset goes through `ModAssetBundleProvider.Reset` to `ModAssetBundleLoader.Reload`, which runs `AssetBundle.Unload(true)` on every mod bundle and loads them again. Only then does the scene teardown run `IUnloadableSingleton.Unload` (`SingletonLifecycleUnityAdapter.OnDestroy`). When `Unload` ran, the Deep Frontier shader variants were already destroyed.
- **Root cause (Unity):** reproduced in Unity 6000.5.11f1 batch mode with a bundle-loaded shader and `Unload(true)`. A material whose shader was destroyed reports `Hidden/InternalErrorShader`, and `== variant` is false. `ShaderSwap.Restore` therefore skipped the restore without any error, and the persistent native water and terrain materials carried the dead shader into the next scene.
- **Fix (`RadiationShading.ShaderSwap`, `RadiationRenderer`):**
  - `Restore` now always puts the native shader and keywords back after `Apply`. It accepts the live variant, or exactly Unity's error shader once the variant has been destroyed, and reports anything else.
  - The renderer registers each swap before applying it, so a PostLoad that fails part-way is restored too.
  - `Unload` restores every swap and then throws if any material held something else. The strict PostLoad check (native shader name, properties, keywords) is unchanged.
- **Exception save (`BuiltinMapSetup.Save`):** the native exception save can run before the first frame, before the placements. When `ErrorReporter.LogString` is set (the first uncaught exception), `Save` now writes outcome `ExceptionSaveBeforeSetup`. That is the world as it is, the same way native `GameStarter`'s starting building is simply missing from such a save, and loading it places nothing. Any other save before setup still throws.
- **Harness:** `-deepFrontierSceneCycle`, run with `tools/start_scene_cycle.ps1` (`Scripts/SceneCycleAcceptance.cs`). It runs save, menu, new game, menu, and so on in one process. Every main menu compares the 2 water and 5 terrain materials with the first menu (same shader object, keywords, every native property value). Every game requires the variants. The launcher backs up Memory Test and restores anything that was removed.
- **In game (build 223, `outputs/scene-cycle-223.log`):**
  - Game 1: Memory Test autosave copy. Game 2: new Canyon game (the user's crash case; map setup placed 17 of 17). Game 3: the save again. All loaded with the variants on all 7 materials.
  - The menus after games 1, 2 and 3 found every material exactly as before the first game, with the same material objects.
  - Game 4 stalled while the window was unfocused (Timberborn does not advance frames without focus), and the user closed it during that load. The native NREs after that close (`SingletonLifecycleService.UnloadSingletons`, `FactionService.Save`) come from a scene destroyed before `LoadAll`, not from Deep Frontier.
  - Memory Test was unchanged. The user also checked by hand.
- **Not run:** the scene-cycle harness to COMPLETE. It needs the window focused for about 12 minutes.

## Construction costs follow materials (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, cost-material agent; data regenerated; validated in mirror `costmat` on HEAD be385c5 + this change; not built or run in game)

- **User rules:** "each building's construction materials must match what the building is visibly made of: its walls, frames and especially its roofs"; "keep in mind the supply chain progression path"; "we don't want to require concrete on a building required to make concrete the first time around". The coordinator relayed the user's decisions: raw recipe effort as value; masonry priced as Sandstone/Limestone; Log in the earliest tier; plaster unpriced; Gears and Machine Cores fixed; quarry masonry unpriced; stage-local values.
- **Rule** (`materialRule` in `building_construction_costs.json`; `tools/material_costs.mjs`; docs/progression.md "Material cost rule"):
  - `lategameCost(id, science)` and the new `buildingCost(id, cost, science)` recompose each cost from the model's measured area per family (wood, metal, concrete, sandstone, limestone). They keep Gear/MachineCore and the gates, and keep the embodied value (amounts round up to 5).
  - The goods come from the tier's `familyGoods` that the settlement can make without the building, at or below its science.
  - Callers now pass the science: 7 generators, `validate_deep_well_content.mjs` and the audit.
- **Measurement:**
  - `tools/asset-pipeline/measure_building_materials.py` decodes the exported finished models (official Timbermesh schema) into `tools/building_material_mix.json`: 48 buildings; structural modules are excluded.
  - Details.IronTeeth is split by atlas region. Stone tiles are assigned by chromaticity to the sandstone/limestone reference tiles; the grey masonry reads as limestone.
  - `node tools/material_costs.mjs --write|--check` records the value table and producer graph in `tools/material_cost_context.json`.
- **Audit** (`audit_progression.mjs`, now loading through the shared `tools/progression_data.mjs`):
  - New explicit `bootstrap-cycle` failure (cost goods producible without the building, at or below its science, cost-gated).
  - Material checks: mix sha256 vs model, context staleness, missing mix, exact goods per rule, family value shares within rounding, and "concrete shown before Concrete exists".
  - No bootstrap cycle exists; a planted Concrete cost on the Concrete Mixer trips it.
- **Numbers (post-roof models):**
  - Stone needed before the quarries: Limestone 1,393 and Sandstone 1,275, against 1,800 in pit range on every built-in map. With the `global` value scope Limestone needed 2,060, so `value.scope` is `buildingWorld`.
  - Concrete up to 4,000 science 1,925 -> 2,915; all buildings 28,815 -> 92,170.
  - Largest changes: Nuclear Reactor 6,265 -> 32,285 Concrete, HighGradeSteel 2,135 -> 1,030; Launch Pad Concrete 3,265 -> 9,040; Radioactive Waste Vault Concrete 910 -> 6,555; quarries 240 Concrete -> none; kiln 145 and mixer 120 Limestone.
- **Checks (mirror, HEAD be385c5 + this change):**
  - Canonical generation is at a fixed point: the mix is unchanged after regeneration, and the context and lategame `--check` pass. Only the 48 building blueprints' BuildingCost changed.
  - `validate_all -SkipBlender`: 55 of 58 pass. `no-game-assets` fails in the mirror (not a git repo) and passes in the repo. `map-setup-terrain` fails only on `map_setup.json smallMap maxArea 11176 != derived 5966`, which comes from the compaction pass's smaller site areas, not costs; the loop coverage passes on every map.
  - Compile check: 0 errors.
- **In game:** not seen yet. Construction menus should show the new costs; nothing else changes at runtime.

## Fix: Deep Mine ore bin top hidden-geometry hole (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, roof-audit agent; scene, export and data promoted; validated in mirror `roofaudit`)

- **Found by** the GPU visibility agent: `audit_hidden_geometry.py` reported 7 culled faces exposed on the Deep Mine, on both the CPU and the GPU path. They were the ore bin's top face (artwork x 6.3..6.6, y 1.5..1.7, z 3.0). The six ore lumps sank 2-3 cm into it, so the enclosed-face cull dropped the top under them while camera rays still reached it between the lumps.
- **Fix** (`lategame_DeepMine.py` `ore_bin`): the lumps rest on the bin top (lowest ring above 3.0), so the top face stays. Audit: 0 of 297 culled faces exposed. Strict z-fight 0 visible at frames 1/31/61/91. Triangles 9,059 -> 9,088 (ceiling re-recorded). Scene unpacked.

## Player-path decks on Fishery Works, Materials Laboratory and Reinforced Hydro Turbine: every flat roof tile placeable (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, roof-audit agent; exports and data promoted; validated in mirror `roofaudit` on HEAD + this change; acceptance fixtures updated and compiled, not run; not built or run in game)

- **Why:** the user wants every flat roof tile to be a placeable area. On these three buildings, 37 flat roof cells sat under BuiltInWalkways path cells ('Floor, Bottom, Path'): Fishery Works galleries and terrace 15, Materials Laboratory terrace 9, Turbine terrace lane 13. `flat_roof_platforms` now passes on every building: 68 templates, 899 flat roof cells, 893 platformable and 6 under solid geometry.
- **Migration:** the same PlayerPlacedPaths contract as the Restaurant, Hospital and Hatchery (`deckMode`, `PlatformDeckSpec`):
  - The outdoor deck cells are free, with stackable slabs under them (`GenerateFloorsOnStackable`).
  - Only the enclosed interior route keeps building navigation edges. Each upper door is a deck door link, and the old route nodes become `suggestedPathCells`.
  - The adaptive rails open on actual player paths (`PlatformDeck`). The exporter writes `<Model>-platform-deck.json` and no more connection-only `WalkwayJoin` planks. The stale `-walkway.json` files are removed.
- **Fishery Works:**
  - The lane in front of the upper hall (5..7,1,1) and the terrace doorstep (2,4,1) share their cells with the hall's front wall and the door house (registered regions). They stay interior building lanes, and `FishFront5..7` are no longer openings, so those rails stay closed.
  - Deck door links: (7,1,1) -> side gallery (8,1,1), and the door house (2,4,1) -> roof terrace (1,4,1).
  - The ground door cell (3,0,0) is an interior cell.
- **Materials Laboratory:** deck door link from the records office (1,3,1) -> terrace (1,2,1).
- **Reinforced Hydro Turbine:** no workers and no enclosed door, so its deck has no door links. Players lay ordinary paths across the terrace, and onto a neighbouring turbine, as on any roof. `PlatformDeck.cs` now accepts an empty (but explicit) door list.
- **Also:**
  - `automation_content.json` drops `BuildingWalkwaySpec`, which no building uses any more. The `BuildingWalkway` component code stays.
  - The aquaculture workbook text was updated.
  - Stale assertion fixed: `validate_hydro_exports.py` size 8x10x7 -> 8x10x6, left over from the 2026-10-30 compaction.
- **Acceptance (C#, compiled with 0 errors, not run):**
  - `FisheryAcceptance`, `ResearchAcceptance` and `HydroAcceptance` check `PlatformDeckSpec` rails: hidden exactly when player paths stand on both sides. They lay a path on each open rail's deck cell and walk grid cells instead of walkway waypoints.
  - The Fishery and Laboratory walk targets moved to layer 1, matching the compaction.
  - `ResearchAcceptance` still expects the pre-compaction 10x8x9 laboratory and its freed cells (pre-existing; not changed here).
- **Old saves:** placed buildings keep their footprint. Their deck cells change from built-in walkway to free stackable cells, so beavers reach the upper doors over player paths or the native deck floors.
- **Checks (mirror):** canonical generation reaches a fixed point. `validate_all -Only` 26 checks pass: exports civic/connection/fishery/hatchery/hydro/research/preview_budget, sources research/hydro/civic, model-bindings, entrances, connection faces, freed cells, roof decks, flat_roof_platforms, smog, status icons, slice content, automation, sounds, construction materials, power rule, workforce risks, audit progression and building assets. The triangle budget passes (878,159 / 922,305) and the incremental compile check passes.

## Flat roofs placeable (new validator), roof variety, no roof vents, no visible concrete before concrete: 17 buildings (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, roof-audit agent; scenes, exports and data promoted; validated in mirror `roofaudit` on HEAD + this change; not built or run in game)

- **User rules:** "all flat roof tiles should be placeable areas, similar to a platform"; roof variety and "no roof vents" (BRIEF "ROOF VARIETY"); buildings that unlock before concrete exists must not show concrete.
- **New check `flat_roof_platforms`** (`tools/validate_flat_roof_platforms.mjs`; validate_all runs every `tools/validate_*.mjs`). A flat roof cell is an occupied cell whose Top face `measure_connection_faces.py` measured flat (the connection-face test, `outputs/connection-faces`), with no block of the building above or with its middle open to the sky. It must be Stackable with a free cell above. The only exemption is solid geometry over the cell: static geometry in the middle of the cell above or anywhere over its middle (`roof.blocked`/`roof.covered`), an animated part there (`roof.animated`), or a smoke/steam mouth in its column (the top of a stack rising through the cell's outer band). Pitched and sloped roofs aren't flat, so they're never offenders. No building list. Report: `outputs/flat-roof-platforms-report.json`; `--table` prints flat / platformable / under solid / offenders.
- **Roof-deck rule** (`roof_deck_content.mjs`): a smoke or steam mouth excludes only its own cell column (`EMITTER_REACH` 1 -> 0.5). A native prop excludes a cell only if its footprint reaches the cell's inner box, 0.31 in from each edge, the band the geometry probe uses (`INNER_INSET`). On HEAD this frees Cement Kiln (4,1,1), Deep Drill (5,5,1), Restaurant (5,3,3) and the two quarries' (2,4..5,0). Other agents' rows stay at HEAD. When the Oil Power Plant's owner regenerates `roof_decks.json`, its (3,2,3)/(4,2,3) get freed as well.
- **Restaurant:** the kitchen stack on the stairhead cap went, with its reservation and smoke. The upper front furniture reservation ends at x 5, because its last cell (5,0,2) holds no furniture, so (5,0,1) is a platformable balcony deck. 0 offenders (was 2).
- **No roof vents:**
  - Removed: the Hospital steam flue and its reservation, the Fishery Works steam chimney and its region, the Grocery rear-slope flue, both Materials Laboratory cowled flues and their regions, and the Deep Drill doghouse stove pipe.
  - Smoke and smog: the Restaurant and Materials Laboratory no longer pollute (their `smog_content.json` entries are removed). `register_smoke_emitters.py` registers the four flueless buildings with an empty list (`FLUELESS`).
  - Moved stacks: the Deep Drill's two smoke stacks, the Deep Mine annex chimney and the Archive's stove flue now rise from the ground or plinth up a wall (`lategame_kit.wall_stack`, no base flare). They stand in the roof cells' outer band, so the decks beside them stay platformable.
- **Roof variety** (late-game buildings, `lategame_roofdeck_parts.deck_slab` boards and sheets, `lategame_mining_parts.plank_deck`):
  - Deep Mine: barracks-roof-plank gable on the lamp house; timber plank deck on the annex.
  - Archive: plank gable on the hall; timber deck on the catalogue wing; the tower stays concrete.
  - Deep Drill: riveted steel plate on the pump-house roof.
  - Deep Ore Processor: steel plate on the casting bay; painted metal sheet on the blower house.
  - Steel Mill: steel plate on the melt shop; painted metal sheet on the teeming bay; the pulpit stays concrete.
  - Industrial roofs: riveted steel plate on the quarry winding house and the Limestone Pit (`industrial_kit.lean_plate_roof`). The Sandstone Pit keeps native roof sheets, and the Concrete Mixer's hopper roof is steel plate.
- **No concrete before concrete:**
  - Stone Crusher: flagstone machine floor (`factory_registration.flagstone_slab`).
  - Concrete Mixer: coursed masonry footings, piers and pedestals (`masonry_box`); stone aggregate; steel roof.
  - Sandstone and Limestone Pit: timber hoist sill, wood and metal only.
  - Fish Hatchery: timber roof slabs, plinth band (`grid_structure.room(plinth=)`), column feet (`timber_column(foot=)`) and pump bed; steel pile shoes (`waterside_supports` `pierMat`).
  - Plaster_White share: Crusher 9.5% -> 0, Mixer 27.5% -> 0, pits 0.7-0.9% -> 0, Hatchery 11.6% -> 0. The Cement Kiln had none.
  - The masonry is the kiln grey/pale masonry (DFIndustrial14/15) in courses or flagstones. Its texture is plain grey noise, so the masonry look comes only from the coursing.
- **Flat roof table, flat / platformable / under solid / offenders (HEAD -> now):** Restaurant 44/42/1/1 -> 45/45/0/0; Limestone and Sandstone Quarry 21/19/0/2 -> 21/21/0/0; Cement Kiln 1/0/1/0 -> 1/1/0/0; Deep Drill 7/6/1/0 -> 9/7/2/0; Deep Mine 21/20/1/0 -> 13/12/1/0 and Archive 20/20/0/0 -> 9/8/1/0 (their gables aren't flat); Deep Processor 23/22/1/0, Steel Mill 49/49, Hospital 20/20, Fish Hatchery 16/16, Grocery 4/4, both pits 8/8 and Stone Crusher 1/1 are unchanged; Oil Power Plant 5/3/2/0 -> 3/3/0/0 (its row regenerated); Rocket Assembly Hangar 14/13/1/0 (row regenerated under the new rule). The Concrete Mixer has no flat roof cell. The Fishery Works (15) and Materials Laboratory (9) gallery and terrace cells stay offenders. They are BuiltInWalkways path cells, and making them placeable means migrating both buildings to PlayerPlacedPaths decks (`PlatformDeckSpec`, as the Restaurant, Hospital and Hatchery were), which needs model rail-join changes and acceptance updates. Not done here.
- **Connection faces:** Deep Drill 153 -> 151, Deep Mine 167 -> 157, Archive 123 -> 105 (gables). The others are unchanged.
- **Triangles** (ceilings re-recorded after review): Deep Mine 8,139 -> 9,059, Archive 8,166 -> 9,034, Deep Ore Processor 6,826 -> 6,946, Steel Mill 9,152 -> 9,314, Crusher 8,769 -> 8,975, Mixer 4,998 -> 5,150. Lower than before: Limestone Pit 8,202 -> 7,538, Materials Laboratory 15,684 -> 14,844, Deep Drill, Restaurant, Hospital, Fishery Works, Grocery and both quarries slightly. The total stays under its ceiling (881,679 / 922,305).
- **Checks:** strict z-fight 0 visible on all 18 scenes (frames 1/31/61/91). Mirror canonical generation on HEAD + this change reaches a fixed point (a second run leaves Data byte-identical). `validate_all -Only`, 35 checks (exports civic/connection/fishery/grocery/hatchery/hospital/industrial/lategame/pit/preview_budget/research, sources mixer/pits/civic, model-bindings, entrances, connection faces, construction materials, freed cells, map setup, power rule, restaurant content/grid, roof decks, smog, status icons, slice content, sounds, automation, rocket program, audit progression/building assets): all pass except `flat_roof_platforms` (Fishery Works 15, Materials Laboratory 9, Reinforced Hydro Turbine 13; deck migration next) and `map-setup-terrain` on `smallMap.maxArea` (known). Triangle budget passes. Scenes saved with `unpack_blend_textures.py`; smoke registrations, wall faces and the review were re-recorded on the unpacked scenes.
- **In game:** not seen yet.

## CPU load: GPU denoising, 32-sample review renders, GPU render lock, hidden-geometry rays in a warm GPU service, faster connection validator (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30; tools only, no game content changed; verified in mirror `gpuaudit`)

- **Where the CPU went:** a per-process sampler over the agents' live workload put ~88% of all CPU in Cycles review renders (`render_lategame_studies.py` 51%, `render_budget_views.py` 37%); the visibility audits were 1-3%.
- **5238cfb** every OptiX renderer denoises on the GPU (`denoising_use_gpu`, fails without an OIDN-capable GPU). ChemicalWorks study, 3 views: 290 -> 37 CPU-s; budget views, 4 views: 257 -> 41 CPU-s; images within 1/255.
- **bc37ac7** review renders at 32 samples (lategame studies, budget views, reference studies); Greenhouse side-by-side against 64 reads the same; render time 35 -> 14 s for three views.
- **4a35c0d** `gpu_render_lock.render()`: one Cycles render at a time machine-wide (named mutex `Local\DeepFrontierGpuRender`, no timeout, released by Windows if the holder dies; crash-tested). A render spins one CPU core for as long as it holds the GPU.
- **675329d, 41ddf02 and the service change:** `audit_hidden_geometry.py` casts its rays on the RTX 2060 (`gpu_visibility.py`, OpenGL compute), reproducing the mathutils BVHTree casts ray for ray (watertight triangle test, BLI_kdopbvh's FLT_EPSILON-grown box culling, float32 arithmetic), inside one warm shared Blender (`gpu_visibility_service.py`, 127.0.0.1:47613) that the unchanged command line submits to and starts when needed. Equivalence: all 94 saved scenes (industrial, civic shells, late-game, structural, map objects with `--terrain-z 0`) give reports identical to the old CPU audit, both per process and through one service lifetime; 99,970/99,970 adversarial one-triangle cases; 0 differences in 600,000 real audit rays. CPU per audit, old -> warm service: Restaurant 15.2 -> 3.1, GroceryMarket 15.5 -> 4.0, Hospital 7.2 -> 2.3, CementKiln 1.9 -> 1.7, tiny scenes 0.7-0.8 -> 0.9-1.1; all 94 scenes 270 -> 158 CPU-s. The DeepMine scene currently fails the audit (7 culled faces exposed) on both paths.
- **3d54a52** `validate_connection_exports.py`: each export decoded once, faces rechecked per direction: ~29 -> ~19 CPU-s per run, output byte-identical.
- **Unchanged:** `zfight_audit.py` (its rays are negligible; its time is Python candidate pairing) and the build-time `enclosed_cull.visible`, now the largest ray cost (14 of 20 CPU-s of a Restaurant build).
- **In game:** not applicable (tooling).

## Compaction: civic and wellbeing late-game buildings (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, built by an agent; each building committed when promoted; validated in mirror `civicwell` on HEAD + that building; not built or run in game)

- **Shared:** `lategame_kit.parapet` (and `flat_roof(..., parapet_height=)`) caps its ring with one IRON plate over the whole roof, so `measure_connection_faces.py` records the roof below as covered and blocked and it never becomes a roof deck. The compacted buildings use `parapet_ring` from the new `lategame_roofdeck_parts.py` instead (walls and cap strips only, inside the 0.15 occupancy inset). The kit helper is unchanged. Costs are held by `lategame_cost_basis.json`.

## Compaction: Stone Crusher one-layer workshop, 4x4x5 -> 4x4x3 (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, built by an agent; scene, export and blueprint promoted; validated in mirror `ind-head` (HEAD + this change) and the real repo; not built or run in game)

- **User:** one layer per storey, flat roofs. The crusher was already 4x4; its workshop was two layers tall under a machine deck at layer 2.
- **Changed:** `CRUSHER_DECK` 2 -> 1 (`industrial_levels.py`): the closed workshop is one layer, the concrete machine floor is at layer 1, and the reviewed machinery (rolls, flywheel, sprockets, chains, hopper, roofs) moves down 1.0 rigidly. The roll and hopper roofs are flat native roof sheets (`crusher_workshop_details.py`), the hopper roof 0.11 above the roll roof. Hard-coded heights that assumed the old deck now follow it: west and east wall braces, couplings, the east cable trunk, the louvre vents. The windows in the one-layer walls are smaller (scale 0.45), and the clerestory pane over the porch went. The porch canopy's back edge and right shoe stop short of the front roll bearings. Height 5 -> 3 (art top 2.92).
- **Kept:** footprint, origin, door, entrance (1,-1,0), materials and atlas slots, animated parts (2 rolls, flywheel, 2 sprockets), recipe, cost and workers (checked against HEAD).
- **Data:** layout size, `industrial_occupancy.mjs` cells (layer 0 workshop and porch, layer 1 machine floor, layer 2 hopper and roofs), catalog size, LEVELS envelope 3; wall faces, connection faces, the `roof_decks.json` row (one platformable cell on the flat hopper roof) and the triangle ceiling (8,817 -> 8,769) remeasured; `IndustrialAcceptance` expects 4x4x3.
- **Cleanup:** `factory_clearance.py` removed (walkway routes for decks that no longer exist; nothing imported it). `test_catalog_surfaces.py` imports work again and its crusher, quarry and pit cases follow the compact geometry; the whole test passes.
- **Old saves:** footprint, origin and entrance unchanged; the top two layers are freed.
- **Checks:** strict z-fight 0 visible; `exports-industrial`, `model-bindings`, `building_entrances`, `construction_materials`, `freed_cells`, `status_icon_anchors`, `audit-building-assets` pass (the `roof_decks` failures are other agents' buildings). Scripts compile.

## Compaction: Restaurant, Hospital and Grocery Store (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, civic agent; generated and validated in mirror `civic`, promoted into Data; not built or run in game)

- **Restaurant 9x7x9 -> 9x7x5** (user: "needs to be shortened but footprint needs to stay the same"; one layer per storey). Footprint, origin and ground door unchanged.
  - Storeys: ground hall, kitchen and vestibule 0-1, middle hall and first dining balcony at 1, upper hall and second balcony at 2, flat roof at 3 (`restaurant_structure.py`, `SIZE`, `HEADROOM` .72 under the deck beams one layer up).
  - Flat roof: the main roof is a `MainRoof` platform (cast slab, timber decking, low parapet) level with the roof terrace bays; the gabled roof, dormers and lantern are gone. The roof stairhead is one layer with a flat cast cap. The carved sign (`#Restaurant.CarvedSign`, 61 frames) swings from a steel gantry on the front edge of the roof; two kitchen chimneys keep their smoke.
  - Kept: all 7 tables and 14 seats, both dining balconies with their rails, planters and lamps; the 3x3 roof terrace stays clear. The oven is rebuilt below the one-layer kitchen's fascia.
  - Interior route: a stair column at (4,3) through the three halls into the stairhead; doors at layers 0/1/2/3. Service cells and staff slots moved to layers 1/2 (`civic_building_content.mjs`). 59 rail openings unchanged.
  - Triangles 19,846 -> 14,622 (ceiling recorded).
- **Hospital 12x9x9 -> 4x9x9** (user: "keep it as it is, but remove the right and left wings, just keeping the center").
  - The central pavilion keeps its roof, storeys, materials, banners and doors exactly; both ward wings, their front balconies, the rear balcony ends, the ward roofs and the West/East ward doors are gone. The rear decks, roof garden and their doors (ground, rear deck, rear ward, roof garden) were already in the pavilion. The pavilion's lower side walls carry the upper storey's lancets now.
  - One steam flue moved onto the roof garden's front-left cell (outside the clear 4x3 area), so the hospital keeps its steam; the two ward flues went with the wings.
  - `ORIGIN` x -4 (artwork keeps its source coordinates); ward work point (6.5,0,3.5) -> (2.5,0,3.5), the same pavilion cell, now from `hospital_layout.wardWorkPosition`.
  - Map variants: each Hospital site moved +4 tiles along its local x, so the pavilion stays where it stood (oasis' door approach had moved onto lower terrain).
  - Triangles 18,222 -> 10,899.
- **Grocery Store 9x6x8 -> 9x6x5**: one layer per storey (shop floor 0-1, market room 1-2, balcony at layer 1, stair bay for one native stair). The gable roof with its lit monitor stays: it is the market's defining silhouette and keeps it clearly different from the flat-roofed Restaurant. Eaves .30 -> .20 so the front and back rows at layer 1 stay free; arcade, stall-wing pents, awning, hoist and stocked displays lowered; the hoist door above the dock and the upper-window flower boxes are gone. Triangles 19,620 -> 18,662.
- **Shared code (append-only; other buildings unchanged):** `grid_structure.room(wall_style='storey')` opt-in one-layer infill; `grid_structure.platform` pier knee braces start above the pier only when `z1-.62` falls inside it (never true for the existing two-layer posts); `civic_validation` takes an explicit per-model `headroom` (1.09 for the two-layer buildings); `civic_isometric_geometry.hanging_board_storey` (new). `validate_building_entrances.mjs` and `civic_building_content.nativeEntrance` accept an Entrance cell whose building block holds only Top/Corners: a vanilla Path ('Path') shares such a cell, as under a vanilla Platform or Overhang (the Restaurant doorstep is under its layer-1 balcony).
- **Saves:** Restaurant and Grocery keep footprint, origin and ground entrance; their upper decks and doors are one/two layers lower, so player paths, stairs and platforms built on the old balconies or terrace float and need re-placing. A placed Hospital's origin is now the pavilion's corner, so an old hospital, flipped or not, shows the pavilion 4 tiles to the left of where it stood (on its old west wing tiles); re-place it and reconnect its shafts and pipes.
- **Checks:** strict z-fight 0 visible, hidden-geometry 0 culled faces exposed (all three), `test_restaurant_shell.py` 55,834 probes 0 uncovered, sources-civic, triangle budget; mirror `civic` (HEAD + this work): canonical generation and `validate_all -SkipBlender` pass for these buildings (exports-civic/hospital/grocery, restaurant grid/content, building entrances, vertical slice, roof decks, connection faces). Other agents' in-progress buildings failed audit-progression (power plant costs) and two beaverome loop sites (ChemicalWorks, PublicHealthLab) in that mirror.
- **Review:** `outputs/compaction/<Building>-before-after.png`, archived in `docs/art/reviews/2026-10-30-compaction-civic/`.
- **Open:** in-game placement, walking and service; the C# acceptance fixtures (Restaurant, Hospital, Grocery) still use the old coordinates.

## Compaction: Sandstone and Limestone Pit, one storey (5x4x4 -> 5x4x3, 5x3x4 -> 5x3x3) (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, built by an agent; scenes, exports and blueprints promoted; validated in mirror `ind-head` (HEAD + this change) and the real repo; not built or run in game)

The jib brace meets the jib clear of the lifted basket. Height 4 -> 3 layers (only the hoist column uses layer 2).

## Compaction: Limestone and Sandstone Quarry 11x7x9 -> 8x6x5 (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, built by an agent; scene, export and blueprints promoted; validated in mirror `ind-head` (HEAD + this change) and the real repo; not built or run in game)

- **User:** smaller buildings, less scaffolding; "the mines need to have a lot of tlc". Shared model `MineralQuarry` (`quarry_isometric_geometry.py`, `quarry_process_details.py`, rewritten).
- **New quarry:** one stone plinth (top at layer 1) over the seam with the lined shaft collar; a four-post timber headframe boarded on its west and rear faces and in an upper band on the front (east side open for the tipping bucket), the head sheave on carriers on its head frame; the winding drum in an open-fronted, flat-roofed winding house behind it, the rope running drum -> sheave -> bucket; receiver, gravity chute, 16-slat belt, vibrating screen and a ground stone bin on the east side; the front cabin with the native door, a flat-roofed tool room on the west, supply bins and a spoil heap on the front strip, the native water tank in front. Flat roofs use the native roof sheets laid level.
- **Removed:** the side ladder mast and its landing, the lattice headframe with its X bracing and bearing gantry, both upper workshops and the control room with their three decorative doors (`LeftWorkshopDoor`, `RightWorkshopDoor`, `ControlRoomDoor` and the `quarry_bay_return` helper; their door-mounting test rows went too), the pipe racks and roof vessels.
- **Kept:** origin, entrance (1,-1,0), the ground cabin door and idle slot, and the 25 seam foundation cells (grid x 3..7, y 1..5, `Top` over `Ground`), so a saved quarry stays over its seam; materials and atlas slots; every animated part name (hoist drum, sheave, carriage, tipping bucket, load, cable, three flywheels, sieve, belt rollers, belt slats (16, were 22), pour/belt/screen stones); recipes, costs, science, power and workers (checked against HEAD).
- **Hoist law:** travel 2.10 (was 3.60), sheave at 4.45; `quarry_validation.py` checks attachment over all 481 frames, minimum rope 0.80 and that the raised yoke stays under the sheave (the ladder-bay rays went with the ladder).
- **Data:** layout size, `industrial_occupancy.mjs` cells, catalog sizes, LEVELS envelope 5; wall faces, connection faces, the two quarry `roof_decks.json` rows and triangle ceilings (11,784 -> 6,967) remeasured; `IndustrialAcceptance` expects 8x6x5 with no open ground cell inside.

- **Maps:** `map-setup-terrain` reports no quarry site error (the seam offset is unchanged). The Beaverome unmet notes in `tools/map_variants` still give the old 11x7 footprint as the reason no quarry site fits; a smaller footprint may now fit there (not re-searched; map data belongs to the map pass).
- **Checks:** strict z-fight 0 visible; motion check; `exports-industrial`, `model-bindings`, `construction_materials`, `freed_cells`, `building_entrances`, `status_icon_anchors`, `exports-preview_budget`, `audit-building-assets` pass; the other failures in that run are other agents' in-progress buildings (Restaurant, Chemical Works, Deep Mine, Public Health Lab, Hospital sites). Scripts compile.

## Compaction: late-game power, nuclear and rocketry buildings (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, built by an agent; promoted building by building; validated in mirror `compactlg`; not built or run in game)

- **Scope:** Nuclear Reactor, Uranium Processing Plant, Radioactive Waste Refinery and Vault, Mission Control, Rocket Assembly Hangar, Launch Pad, Coal and Oil Power Plants, the six small radiation/bot buildings and the Reinforced Hydro Turbine (one agent). Each building is committed as soon as it is promoted. Shared rules: `outputs/compaction/BRIEF.md`. The cost of every compacted late-game building is held by `tools/lategame_cost_basis.json` (46d9ee9).
- **Process per building:** the old scene is copied to `outputs/compaction/before/`; the generator is rewritten and the scene saved unpacked; matched before/after cameras go to `outputs/compaction/<Id>-before-after.png`; strict z-fight audit at frames 1/31/61/91; `lategame_grid_audit`; official export. Canonical generation, connection faces and roof decks run in mirror `compactlg`, which is HEAD plus this work only (other agents' uncommitted files and scenes reset to HEAD). Only these buildings' files and their keys in `roof_decks.json` and `triangle-budget.json` are promoted.
- **Coal Power Plant** 5x5x10 -> 4x4x6 (`lategame_CoalPowerPlant.py`). Boiler house on tiles 0..3 x 0..4: concrete ground storey (layer 0) and one steel-clad storey (layer 1) under a flat concrete roof with an open parapet (top at layer 3), with the riveted coal bunker standing on it. The plank gable is gone. Engine deck (layer 1) and chimney base on tile column 3; the chimney rises to 5.9 (it was 9.9). Flywheel and piston rod keep their names. Triangles 3,758 -> 2,572.
  - **Data:** same origin, door and Entrance (1,-1,0) / LocalAccess. Shaft faces 87 -> 55, pipe ports 62 -> 36, colliders 10 -> 4, 3 roof-deck cells. Smoke and smog follow the stack, now at (3.52, 3.02, 5.9). Cost (125 Plank, 75 Gear, 75 MetalBlock, 30 TreatedPlank, 100 Concrete) and output (25,000) unchanged.
- **Oil Power Plant** 6x6x9 -> 5x4x6 (`lategame_OilPowerPlant.py`). Turbine hall on tiles 0..5 x 0..2: concrete ground storey and one steel-clad storey under a flat roof (top at layer 3). Its two fin-fan coolers now spin in cowls on that roof; the barrel vault and the four-column cooler frame over the tanks are gone. Tank bund with two day tanks and the transfer pump on tiles 0..3 x 2..4. Boiler tower on tiles 3..5 x 2..4 to layer 4, with its two stacks to 5.84. The three animated parts keep their names. `validate_connection_exports.py` CURVED_CASES: the vault faces are replaced by the arched door portal. Triangles 7,160 -> 4,240.
  - **Data:** same origin, door and Entrance (1,-1,0). Shaft faces 125 -> 73, pipe ports 88 -> 50, colliders 4 -> 6, 3 roof-deck cells. Smoke at (3.55 / 4.45, 3.1, 5.84). Cost and output (90,000) unchanged.
- **Old saves (both plants):** a placed plant keeps its origin and entrance. The cells it no longer uses become empty ground. A flipped plant's entrance moves one tile (local x 3 -> 2 for Coal, 4 -> 3 for Oil). Shafts and pipes on removed walls disconnect and need reconnecting.
- **Checks (mirror, Coal and Oil):** canonical generation; `validate_all -Only` exports-connection, exports-lategame, exports-preview_budget, model-bindings, building_entrances, connection_faces, freed_cells, lategame_freed_cells, map_setup, power_rule, roof_decks, smog_content, status_icon_anchors, vertical_slice_content, audit-progression and audit-building-assets pass. Strict z-fight audit: 0 visible. `map-setup-terrain` fails only on `smallMap.maxArea` (re-derived once the pass is done, as on HEAD).
- **Uranium Processing Plant** 12x11x14 -> 8x7x8 (`lategame_UraniumProcessingPlant.py`).
  - **Building:** a one-storey concrete podium with the door, and on its deck an open row of five spinning centrifuges under a solid flat canopy. The cascade hall behind them is lit by a flat-roofed glazed clerestory monitor; roof parapets are open `parapet_ring`s.
  - **Around it:** a 2x2 thickener with its rake, the fuel vault, two acid tanks on a concrete bund, and the scrubber house with its column, fan and a stack to 7.8.

Under the no-clutter rule, the open drum dock and its jib hoist also went, so `HoistCable` and `HoistHook` are dropped and tiles 6..8 x 2..4 are free.

Shaft faces 439 -> 267, pipe ports 126 -> 76 (the inlet (0,0,0) Down first), colliders 94 -> 35, roof-deck cells 53 -> 55. towerSteam emitters at the new tower mouths (2 / 12, 7.6, 7). ## Compaction: Deep Mine 10x11x16 -> 7x6x7 (Claude Code session, 2026-10-30, built by an agent; scene, export and blueprint promoted; validated in mirror `minesdrill` on HEAD b2d8fb6 + this change; not built or run in game)

- **Art** (`lategame_DeepMine.py`, saved scene unpacked; user: "the mines need to have a lot of tlc"): a compact plated steel headframe (head deck at layer 5) on a low one-layer concrete shaft house over the vein. Corrugated sides and back, open front showing both skips on their timber guides, two box-girder backstays, two 1.2-tile sheave wheels on the head deck. The winding drum and flywheel stand in the open on the shaft-house deck, their ropes running up to the sheaves. Ore flow: the skips tip into a chute through the headframe's side, into a boarded ore bin; its front chute fills an ore tub that runs out along a short front track. Brick lamp house with the door and a flat parapet roof; one-layer bath-house annex with chimney and hot-water tank. Removed: the 13-layer open lattice headframe (bracing, gussets, lattice backstays, gantry, caged ladder), the drum shed, the two-storey monitor-roofed engine house, the ore bin on stilts with its tipple deck and head-house, and the gabled bath house. Same textures, material slots and UV authoring.
- **Animation:** the 8 animated parts keep their names (sheaves, skips, ropes, drum, flywheel); skip travel 2.6 (was 7.6). New ninth part `OreTub` (SLIDE_X 1.5).
- **Checks on the art:** triangles 18,508 -> 9,436 (ceiling re-recorded); strict z-fight audit 0 visible at frames 1/26/51/61/91/111; grid audit 0 edge and floor misses, nothing floating; back-face-culled renders show no holes.
- **Data:** footprint inside the old one with the same origin, door tile, Entrance (1,-1,0) and understructure core (tiles x 2-6, y 1-5, `DeepOreVein`): all 25 core cells are building cells (Top), so the vein still has one alignment and map placements (`understructure` = mine + (2,1)) stay valid. Door face on the tile edge (`LocalAccess` z 0.18 -> 0). Shaft transputs 133 -> 85, pipe ports 77 -> 41; smoke and smog at the engine exhaust (4.5, 5.55) and the bath-house chimney (0.55, 5.45); status icon 7.7. Roof decks: 18 platformable cells (lamp-house roof, bath-house roof, the deck, one head-deck cell). Cost held by `lategame_cost_basis.json`; power, 6 workers, recipe, drill bits and shaft lining unchanged. `lategame_freed_cells.json`: the 2026-10-22 inside cells now lie outside the 7x6 footprint (footprintAfter 7x6x7, no inside cells).
- **Old saves:** a placed mine keeps its position and vein and frees the tiles it no longer uses, at any rotation. Flipped mines are the exception: a flip mirrors the footprint across its width, so a flipped mine's entrance moves 3 tiles (local x 8 -> 5) and its vein core moves from local x 3-7 to 0-4, off the vein it was placed on. Re-place a flipped Deep Mine (no map variant places one flipped). Shafts and pipes on removed walls disconnect.
- **Maps:** `map-setup-terrain` still fails only on `smallMap.maxArea` (derived now 10,328), re-derived after the pass. Diorama's unmet Deep Mine reason was measured with the 10x11 mine; a 7x6 mine may now fit (left to the map owner).
- **Review:** `outputs/compaction/DeepMine-before-after.png`, `DeepMine-compaction-close.png`, `DeepMine-footprint-height.png`.
- **Checks (mirror):** canonical generation to a fixed point; the same 19 `validate_all -Only` checks as the drill: 18 pass, `map-setup-terrain` as above; triangle budget passes.
- **In game:** not seen yet.
